User: Round 3.14159 to three significant figures.
Joe: The first three significant figures are 3, 1, and 4. The next digit is 1, which is less than 5, so we round down. The answer is 3.14.
User: What about rounding the same number to two decimal places?
Joe: Two decimal places means we look at the third decimal, which is 1. Since 1 is less than 5, we round down to 3.14.

User: What is the order of magnitude of 5,000,000?
Joe: 5,000,000 is 5 * 10^6, so its order of magnitude is 10^6.
User: And what about 0.000042?
Joe: 0.000042 = 4.2 * 10^-5, so its order of magnitude is 10^-5.

User: A website says the Eiffel Tower weighs 7,300 tons. Is that plausible?
Joe: The Eiffel Tower is made of wrought iron with density about 8 tons per cubic meter. 7,300 tons would be about 900 cubic meters of iron. A rough volume check: base area 125 m by 125 m, average thickness 0.1 m gives about 1,500 cubic meters, so 7,300 tons is plausible for a lattice structure.
User: How much would a solid iron tower of the same size weigh?
Joe: A solid iron tower the size of the Eiffel Tower (300 m tall, average cross-section roughly 100 sq m) would have about 30,000 cubic meters of iron, weighing around 240,000 tons. The actual tower is much lighter because it is an open lattice.

User: Estimate how many breaths a person takes per day.
Joe: A person breathes about 12-16 times per minute at rest. Using 15 breaths per minute times 60 minutes times 24 hours gives about 21,600 breaths per day.
User: How many in an average lifetime of 80 years?
Joe: 21,600 breaths per day times 365 days times 80 years equals about 630 million breaths in a lifetime.

User: Multiply 2.5 and 3.4, giving the answer with the correct number of significant figures.
Joe: 2.5 * 3.4 = 8.5. Both numbers have 2 significant figures, so the answer should have 2 significant figures. 8.5 is correct.
User: What is 0.004 * 250, with sig figs?
Joe: 0.004 has 1 significant figure and 250 has 2 or 3. The least precise is 1 sig fig, so 0.004 * 250 = 1, which is 1 sig fig.

User: Estimate the square root of 67.
Joe: 8^2 = 64 and 9^2 = 81, so sqrt(67) is between 8 and 9. Since 67 is closer to 64, the estimate is about 8.2.
User: How about the cube root of 130?
Joe: 5^3 = 125 and 6^3 = 216, so cube root of 130 is about 5.05, very close to 5.

User: Is it plausible that a car uses 5 liters of fuel per 100 km?
Joe: Yes, that is 47 mpg, which is reasonable for a modern hybrid car. A typical gasoline car uses 7-10 liters per 100 km, while hybrids can achieve 4-5 liters per 100 km.
User: What about 20 liters per 100 km?
Joe: 20 L/100 km is about 12 mpg, which is typical for a large SUV or truck. For a compact car that would be very inefficient, but for a heavy vehicle it is plausible.

User: Estimate 31 * 49 without a calculator.
Joe: Round 31 to 30 and 49 to 50, so 30 * 50 = 1500. The actual answer is 31 * 49 = 31 * 50 - 31 = 1550 - 31 = 1519, so 1500 is a good estimate.
User: What is 31 * 51?
Joe: 31 * 51 = 31 * 50 + 31 = 1550 + 31 = 1581. Or notice 31 * 51 = (30 * 51) + 51 = 1530 + 51 = 1581.

User: What is the order of magnitude of the speed of light in km/h?
Joe: The speed of light is about 300,000 km/s or 1.08 * 10^9 km/h. The order of magnitude is 10^9 km/h.
User: How does that compare to a car's speed?
Joe: A car at 100 km/h is 10^2 km/h. The speed of light is about 10^7 times faster than a car.

User: A phone costs $800. You pay $200 down and $25 per month. How many months to pay off?
Joe: Remaining after down payment = 800 - 200 = 600. Months = 600 / 25 = 24 months.
User: How much total interest if the cash price is $720?
Joe: You pay 200 + 24 * 25 = 200 + 600 = 800. The cash price is 720. Interest = 800 - 720 = $80.

User: Estimate how many steps a person walks in a lifetime.
Joe: A person walks about 5,000 to 10,000 steps per day. Using 7,000 steps per day times 365 days times 80 years gives about 204 million steps.
User: How many miles is that?
Joe: Average stride is about 2.5 feet, so 204 million steps * 2.5 feet = 510 million feet. Dividing by 5,280 feet per mile gives about 96,600 miles, roughly 4 times around the Earth.

User: What is 1.234 + 5.6, with correct significant figures?
Joe: 1.234 + 5.6 = 6.834. The least precise decimal place is the tenths place from 5.6. So the answer should be rounded to 6.8.
User: What about 12.345 - 0.6?
Joe: 12.345 - 0.6 = 11.745. The least precise is the tenths place, so the answer is 11.7.

User: Estimate the total cost of 17 items at $19.95 each.
Joe: Round 17 to 20 and $19.95 to $20, so 20 * 20 = 400. The actual cost is 17 * 19.95 = 339.15. The estimate of 400 is a bit high.
User: How could you get a better estimate?
Joe: Use 17 * 20 = 340 and subtract 17 * 0.05 = 0.85 to get 339.15 exactly. Or round to 20 * 20 = 400 and subtract 3 * 20 = 60 and 17 * 0.05 = 0.85, giving 339.15.

User: A news article says a new skyscraper weighs 500,000 tons. Is that reasonable?
Joe: The Burj Khalifa weighs about 500,000 tons. A typical skyscraper weighs around 200,000 to 500,000 tons. So yes, that is reasonable for a very large building.
User: How much does a typical house weigh?
Joe: A typical wood-frame house weighs about 80 to 100 tons. A brick house is heavier, around 150 to 200 tons.

User: How many gallons of gasoline does the US use per day?
Joe: The US uses about 140 billion gallons of gasoline per year. Dividing by 365 gives roughly 380 million gallons per day.
User: How many cars would that fuel?
Joe: If the average car uses about 500 gallons per year, 140 billion gallons would fuel 280 million cars, which is close to the number of cars in the US.

User: How many pizzas are consumed in the US per year?
Joe: There are about 330 million people. Assume each person eats pizza once a month on average. That gives 330 million * 12 = about 4 billion pizzas per year.
User: How many pizza restaurants does that support?
Joe: If a pizza restaurant sells 200 pizzas per day times 365 days = 73,000 pizzas per year. Then 4 billion / 73,000 = about 55,000 pizza restaurants, which is close to the actual number.

User: A store has a 30% off sale, then takes an extra 15% off at the register. What is the total discount?
Joe: The price after 30% off is 70% of original. Then 15% off that gives 70% * 85% = 59.5% of original. The total discount is 100% - 59.5% = 40.5%.
User: If the original price was $200, what is the final price?
Joe: 200 * 0.595 = $119. Or step by step: 200 * 0.70 = 140, then 140 * 0.85 = 119.

User: Round 5,499 to the nearest thousand and to the nearest hundred.
Joe: To the nearest thousand, 5,499 is closer to 5,000 than 6,000, so 5,000. To the nearest hundred, it is closer to 5,500 than 5,400, so 5,500.
User: What is the difference between the two rounded values?
Joe: The difference between 5,500 and 5,000 is 500.

User: What is the population density of New York City?
Joe: NYC has about 8.4 million people in 300 square miles, giving about 28,000 people per square mile. Manhattan alone is about 70,000 per square mile.
User: How much space does each person get in Manhattan?
Joe: 70,000 people per square mile means each person has about 1 / 70,000 square miles. In square feet, that is (27,878,400 sq ft per sq mile) / 70,000 = about 400 square feet per person.

User: You roll a die 3 times. What is the probability of rolling at least one 5?
Joe: P(no 5) = (5/6)^3 = 125/216. P(at least one 5) = 1 - 125/216 = 91/216.
User: What is the probability of rolling exactly two 5s?
Joe: There are C(3,2) = 3 ways to choose which rolls are 5s. Each way has probability (1/6)^2 * (5/6) = 5/216. So P = 3 * 5/216 = 15/216 = 5/72.

User: Estimate how long it takes money to double at 7% annual interest.
Joe: Using the Rule of 72, 72 / 7 = about 10.3 years. So money doubles in roughly 10 years at 7% interest.
User: What about at 5%?
Joe: 72 / 5 = about 14.4 years. At 10% it would be 72 / 10 = 7.2 years.

User: What is the order of magnitude of the number of cells in the human body?
Joe: The human body has about 37 trillion cells, which is 3.7 * 10^13. The order of magnitude is 10^13.
User: How about the number of bacteria in the human body?
Joe: There are about 39 trillion bacteria in the human body, which is also 10^13, roughly the same order of magnitude as human cells.

User: How many words does a person speak in a day?
Joe: The average person speaks about 16,000 words per day. At 150 words per minute, that is about 107 minutes of talking per day.
User: How many words in a lifetime?
Joe: 16,000 words per day * 365 days * 80 years = about 467 million words in a lifetime.

User: How much water does a typical household use per year?
Joe: The average US household uses about 300 gallons per day. That is 300 * 365 = about 110,000 gallons per year.
User: How many Olympic swimming pools would that fill?
Joe: An Olympic pool holds about 660,000 gallons. So one household's annual usage is about 1/6 of an Olympic pool.

User: What is 12.0 / 3.00, with sig figs?
Joe: 12.0 / 3.00 = 4.00. Both numbers have 3 significant figures, so the answer should have 3 sig figs. The exact result is 4, so we write 4.00.
User: What about 12 / 3.00?
Joe: 12 has 2 sig figs, 3.00 has 3. The answer should have 2 sig figs. 12 / 3.00 = 4.0.

User: What is the order of magnitude of the world population?
Joe: The world population is about 8.1 billion, which is 8.1 * 10^9. The order of magnitude is 10^9.
User: What about the GDP of the US in dollars?
Joe: The US GDP is about 25 trillion dollars, or 2.5 * 10^13. The order of magnitude is 10^13.

User: A baker uses 2.5 cups of flour for 12 muffins. How much flour for 30 muffins?
Joe: Muffin ratio = 30/12 = 2.5. Flour needed = 2.5 * 2.5 = 6.25 cups.
User: If flour costs $0.40 per cup, what is the flour cost for 30 muffins?
Joe: 6.25 * 0.40 = $2.50.

User: How many sheets of paper does a typical office worker use per year?
Joe: An office worker uses about 10,000 sheets per year, or about 40 sheets per day. A typical case of paper has 5,000 sheets, so that is 2 cases per year per worker.
User: How many trees does that require?
Joe: One tree produces about 8,000 sheets of paper. So one worker uses about 1.25 trees worth of paper per year.

User: How many times does a human heart beat in a lifetime?
Joe: The heart beats about 70 times per minute. 70 * 60 * 24 * 365 * 80 = about 2.9 billion heartbeats in 80 years.
User: How many gallons of blood does it pump?
Joe: The heart pumps about 5 liters per minute. 5 L/min * 60 * 24 * 365 * 80 = about 210 million liters. Dividing by 3.785 L/gallon gives about 55 million gallons.

User: How long would it take to count to a billion?
Joe: If you say one number per second, it would take 1 billion seconds. Dividing by 86,400 seconds per day gives about 11,574 days, or 31.7 years.
User: What about counting to a trillion?
Joe: At one number per second, a trillion seconds is 31,688 years. That is longer than recorded history.

User: A rectangular field is 3 times as long as it is wide. Its perimeter is 400 meters. What is its area?
Joe: Let width = w, length = 3w. Perimeter = 2(w + 3w) = 8w = 400, so w = 50 m, length = 150 m. Area = 150 * 50 = 7,500 sq m.
User: How many hectares is that?
Joe: 1 hectare = 10,000 sq m. So 7,500 / 10,000 = 0.75 hectares.

User: Calculate 2.5 * 3.678 and give the answer with proper sig figs.
Joe: 2.5 * 3.678 = 9.195. 2.5 has 2 sig figs, 3.678 has 4. The answer should have 2 sig figs, so 9.2.
User: What if both numbers are exact?
Joe: If both are exact counts with no measurement uncertainty, the product is exactly 9.195 and sig figs do not apply.

User: Is it plausible that a person runs a marathon in under 2 hours?
Joe: The world record is about 2 hours 35 seconds. A sub-2-hour marathon is not yet achieved in official competition. Eliud Kipchoge ran 1:59:40 in a controlled event with pacers, so it is at the edge of plausibility for elite runners under ideal conditions.
User: What is the average marathon time?
Joe: The average marathon finish time is about 4.5 hours for men and 5 hours for women. Sub-2 is only plausible for a handful of elite athletes.

User: Round 2.675 to two decimal places.
Joe: 2.675 to two decimal places: the third decimal is 5, so we round up. The answer is 2.68.
User: What about 2.6749?
Joe: The third decimal is 4, so we round down. 2.6749 to two decimal places is 2.67.

User: How many kernels of popcorn are in a standard bag of microwave popcorn?
Joe: A standard bag contains about 1/3 cup of kernels. There are roughly 800 kernels per cup, so about 270 kernels per bag.
User: How many popped pieces fit in a 1-liter bowl?
Joe: Popped popcorn has a volume about 40 times the kernel volume. 270 kernels would fill about 270 * 40 / 800 = 13.5 cups, or about 3.2 liters.

User: Solve for x and y: x + y = 12 and x - y = 4.
Joe: Adding the equations gives 2x = 16, so x = 8. Then y = 12 - 8 = 4. The solution is x = 8, y = 4.
User: What is x * y?
Joe: x * y = 8 * 4 = 32.

User: Estimate the value of pi * 25.
Joe: pi is about 3.14, so 3.14 * 25 = 78.5. A quick estimate: 3 * 25 = 75, plus a little more, so about 78.
User: What about pi * 50?
Joe: 3.14 * 50 = 157. Or double the previous answer: 78.5 * 2 = 157.

User: Estimate how many bricks are in a typical house.
Joe: A 2,000 sq ft house has about 6,000 sq ft of wall area. With bricks at about 7 per sq ft for a single layer, that is 42,000 bricks. But most houses use bricks only as a veneer, not structural.
User: How much would those bricks weigh?
Joe: Each brick weighs about 5 pounds. 42,000 bricks * 5 pounds = 210,000 pounds or 105 tons.

User: What is the sum of 1,234 and 56.7 with proper sig figs?
Joe: 1,234 + 56.7 = 1,290.7. The least precise is 1,234 which has uncertainty in the ones place. So the answer should be rounded to the ones place: 1,291.
User: What if the numbers are 1,234.0 and 56.7?
Joe: 1,234.0 has precision to the tenths place. The sum is 1,290.7, and with the least precise being the tenths place, the answer is 1,290.7.

User: How many McDonald's restaurants are there in the US?
Joe: There are about 13,000 McDonald's in the US. With 330 million people, that is one restaurant per 25,000 people.
User: How many burgers do they sell per day?
Joe: McDonald's sells about 2.5 billion burgers per year worldwide. The US share is about half, so 1.25 billion per year, or about 3.4 million per day across all US locations.

User: If a car travels at 55 mph, how many feet per second is that?
Joe: 55 mph = 55 * 5,280 feet per hour = 290,400 feet per hour. Dividing by 3,600 seconds gives about 80.7 feet per second.
User: How many car lengths per second at that speed?
Joe: An average car is about 15 feet long. 80.7 / 15 = about 5.4 car lengths per second.

User: Is it plausible that a human can drink 5 gallons of water in a day?
Joe: No, that is dangerous. The maximum safe water intake is about 1 gallon per day for an adult. Drinking 5 gallons could cause water intoxication and hyponatremia, which can be fatal.
User: What about 5 gallons of soda?
Joe: Even worse. 5 gallons of soda contains about 2,400 grams of sugar, which is 60 times the recommended daily limit. It would cause severe hyperglycemia and likely hospitalization.

User: A book has 250 pages. You read 30 pages on the first day and 15 more pages each subsequent day. How many days to finish?
Joe: After day 1, remaining = 250 - 30 = 220 pages. At 15 pages per day, 220 / 15 = 14.67, so 15 more days. Total = 1 + 15 = 16 days.
User: How many total pages will you have read by the end of day 10?
Joe: Day 1: 30 pages. Days 2-10: 9 days * 15 = 135 pages. Total = 30 + 135 = 165 pages.

User: How many students are in a typical US high school?
Joe: A typical US high school has about 1,500 to 2,000 students. Large urban schools can have 3,000 to 4,000, while small rural schools may have 200 to 500.
User: How many teachers are needed?
Joe: With a student-to-teacher ratio of about 20:1, a school of 1,500 students needs about 75 teachers.

User: Round 3,456 to the nearest hundred and nearest ten.
Joe: To the nearest hundred, 3,456 is between 3,400 and 3,500. The tens digit is 5, so we round up to 3,500. To the nearest ten, the ones digit is 6, so we round up to 3,460.
User: What is the difference between the two rounded values?
Joe: 3,500 - 3,460 = 40.

User: Two cars start from the same point. One goes east at 60 mph, the other goes north at 80 mph. How fast is the distance between them increasing after 1 hour?
Joe: After t hours, the east car has gone 60t miles and the north car 80t miles. The distance between them is sqrt((60t)^2 + (80t)^2) = 100t miles. So the distance increases at 100 mph.
User: How far apart are they after 2.5 hours?
Joe: 100 * 2.5 = 250 miles.

User: Calculate 0.05 * 0.05 with proper sig figs.
Joe: 0.05 has 1 significant figure. 0.05 * 0.05 = 0.0025. With 1 sig fig, the answer is 0.003.
User: What about 0.050 * 0.050?
Joe: 0.050 has 2 sig figs. 0.050 * 0.050 = 0.0025, and with 2 sig figs the answer is 0.0025.

User: Estimate the area of Texas in square miles.
Joe: Texas is about 800 miles from east to west and 800 miles from north to south. A rough estimate is 800 * 800 = 640,000 square miles. The actual area is 268,597 square miles, so the rectangle estimate is too large.
User: How could you improve the estimate?
Joe: Texas is roughly a trapezoid. Use (top + bottom) / 2 * height = (200 + 800) / 2 * 700 = 500 * 700 = 350,000. Still an overestimate but closer.

User: Is it plausible that a 100-watt light bulb uses 100 watts of electricity?
Joe: Yes, that is exactly what the rating means. A 100-watt bulb uses 100 joules of energy per second. Over 1 hour, it uses 100 watt-hours or 0.1 kilowatt-hours.
User: How much does it cost to run for 24 hours at $0.12 per kWh?
Joe: 100 watts * 24 hours = 2,400 watt-hours = 2.4 kWh. Cost = 2.4 * 0.12 = $0.29.

User: What is the order of magnitude of the Planck constant in SI units?
Joe: The Planck constant is 6.626 * 10^-34 J-s. The order of magnitude is 10^-34.
User: How does that compare to the mass of an electron?
Joe: The electron mass is 9.109 * 10^-31 kg, which is 10^-30. The Planck constant is about 10^4 times smaller in its order of magnitude.

User: Estimate 7/8 + 5/6 without a common denominator.
Joe: 7/8 is close to 0.875 and 5/6 is about 0.833. Together that is about 1.708. The exact answer is 7/8 + 5/6 = 21/24 + 20/24 = 41/24 = 1 17/24.
User: Estimate 7/8 - 5/6.
Joe: 7/8 = 21/24 and 5/6 = 20/24, so the exact difference is 1/24, which is about 0.042.

User: How many cups of coffee are consumed worldwide per day?
Joe: About 2.25 billion cups of coffee are consumed per day worldwide. The US alone consumes about 400 million cups per day.
User: How much coffee is that per person per year in the US?
Joe: 400 million cups per day * 365 days = 146 billion cups per year. Divided by 330 million people gives about 442 cups per person per year, or about 1.2 cups per day per person.

User: A circle has circumference 31.4 cm. What is its radius? Use pi = 3.14.
Joe: Circumference = 2 * pi * r = 31.4, so 2 * 3.14 * r = 31.4, so 6.28 * r = 31.4, so r = 31.4 / 6.28 = 5 cm.
User: What is its area?
Joe: Area = pi * r^2 = 3.14 * 25 = 78.5 sq cm.

User: Round 149,600,000 to two significant figures.
Joe: 149,600,000 has 4 sig figs. To 2 sig figs: the first two digits are 1 and 4, the third is 9, so round up. 150,000,000 or 1.5 * 10^8.
User: What is this number? It is the distance from Earth to the Sun in km.
Joe: Yes, 1 AU = 149,600,000 km, or about 150 million km.

User: How long would it take to fill an Olympic swimming pool with a garden hose?
Joe: An Olympic pool holds about 2.5 million liters. A garden hose flows at about 10 liters per minute. Time = 2,500,000 / 10 = 250,000 minutes = about 174 days.
User: What if you used a fire hose?
Joe: A fire hose flows at about 1,000 liters per minute. Time = 2,500,000 / 1,000 = 2,500 minutes = about 42 hours.

User: Is it plausible that the Great Pyramid of Giza was built in 20 years?
Joe: Yes, historical records and archaeological evidence indicate it was built over about 20 years during the reign of Pharaoh Khufu around 2560 BC. It contains about 2.3 million stone blocks.
User: How many blocks per day is that?
Joe: 2.3 million blocks / (20 years * 365 days) = about 315 blocks per day. Working 10 hours per day, that is about 31 blocks per hour, or one block every 2 minutes. That is plausible for a large workforce.

User: A triangle has vertices at (0, 0), (6, 0), and (0, 8). What is its area?
Joe: This is a right triangle with legs 6 and 8. Area = (1/2) * 6 * 8 = 24.
User: What is the length of the median from the right angle?
Joe: The median from the right angle goes to the midpoint of the hypotenuse. The hypotenuse endpoints are (6,0) and (0,8), so midpoint is (3,4). The distance from (0,0) to (3,4) is sqrt(9 + 16) = 5.

User: Estimate the surface area of a human body.
Joe: The average adult has about 1.7 to 1.9 square meters of skin surface area. A common formula uses weight and height, but a rough estimate is about 2 square meters.
User: How much does all that skin weigh?
Joe: Skin weighs about 3.5 kg on average, making it the largest organ by weight. It accounts for about 5% of body weight.

User: A rectangle measures 3.2 cm by 4.56 cm. What is its area with proper sig figs?
Joe: 3.2 * 4.56 = 14.592. 3.2 has 2 sig figs, 4.56 has 3. The answer should have 2 sig figs: 15 sq cm.
User: What about the perimeter?
Joe: Perimeter = 2(3.2 + 4.56) = 2 * 7.76 = 15.52. The least precise is 3.2 (tenths place), so the answer is 15.5 cm.

User: A store sells a jacket for $80, which is a 20% markup from cost. What was the cost?
Joe: Selling price = cost * 1.20 = 80, so cost = 80 / 1.20 = $66.67.
User: What is the profit margin as a percentage of cost?
Joe: Profit = 80 - 66.67 = 13.33. Margin on cost = 13.33 / 66.67 = 0.20 = 20%.

User: How many calories does an average person consume in a year?
Joe: The average adult consumes about 2,000 to 2,500 calories per day. Using 2,200 per day, that is 2,200 * 365 = about 803,000 calories per year.
User: How many pounds of food is that?
Joe: Food has about 1,000 calories per pound on average. So 803,000 / 1,000 = about 803 pounds of food per year.

User: If 8 workers can build a wall in 6 days, how many workers are needed to build the same wall in 4 days?
Joe: Total work = 8 workers * 6 days = 48 worker-days. For 4 days: 48 / 4 = 12 workers.
User: How long would 6 workers take?
Joe: 48 / 6 = 8 days.

User: Estimate the volume of a human body in liters.
Joe: The average human body has about the same density as water, which is 1 kg per liter. An average 70 kg person has a volume of about 70 liters.
User: How many people could fit in a standard 20-foot shipping container?
Joe: A 20-foot container is about 33 cubic meters, which is 33,000 liters. 33,000 / 70 = about 470 people, but they would not fit due to shape constraints. Realistically, about 150 to 200 people standing.

User: What is 9.99 * 8.88 with proper sig figs?
Joe: 9.99 * 8.88 = 88.7112. Both numbers have 3 sig figs, so the answer should have 3 sig figs: 88.7.
User: What about 9.99 + 8.88?
Joe: 9.99 + 8.88 = 18.87. Both have precision to the hundredths place, so the answer is 18.87.

User: What is the order of magnitude of the radius of a hydrogen atom?
Joe: The Bohr radius is about 5.29 * 10^-11 meters. The order of magnitude is 10^-11 meters.
User: How does that compare to the radius of a proton?
Joe: The proton radius is about 8.4 * 10^-16 meters, which is 10^-15. The hydrogen atom is about 10^4 times larger than the proton.

User: Round 0.9999 to three decimal places.
Joe: 0.9999 to three decimal places: look at the fourth decimal, which is 9, so round up. 1.000.
User: Round 0.9994 to three decimal places.
Joe: The fourth decimal is 4, so round down. 0.999.

User: How many trees are on Earth?
Joe: There are about 3 trillion trees on Earth, according to a 2015 study. That is about 420 trees per person.
User: How many trees are cut down per year?
Joe: About 15 billion trees are cut down each year. That is about 4 trees per person per year, but only about 5 billion are replanted.

User: Is it plausible that a person can survive without food for 60 days?
Joe: Yes, with water, a person can survive 30 to 60 days without food, depending on body fat reserves. The longest recorded fast was 382 days under medical supervision.
User: What about without water?
Joe: No. A person can survive only about 3 to 5 days without water. The body needs water for all metabolic processes.

User: The sum of two numbers is 30. The larger is 4 times the smaller. What are the numbers?
Joe: Let smaller = x, larger = 4x. Then x + 4x = 30, so 5x = 30, x = 6. The numbers are 6 and 24.
User: What is the average of the two numbers?
Joe: Average = (6 + 24) / 2 = 30 / 2 = 15.

User: How much energy does a person use per day in food calories?
Joe: The average person consumes about 2,200 food calories per day. One food calorie is 1,000 chemistry calories or 4,184 joules. So that is about 9.2 million joules per day.
User: How does that compare to a car?
Joe: A gallon of gasoline contains about 120 million joules. So a person's daily energy is equivalent to about 0.077 gallons of gas, or about 1.3 cups.

User: Estimate how many minutes a year has.
Joe: 365 days * 24 hours * 60 minutes = 525,600 minutes. A leap year has 366 * 24 * 60 = 527,040 minutes.
User: How many seconds in a year?
Joe: 525,600 * 60 = 31,536,000 seconds in a common year, or about pi * 10^7 seconds.

User: A cube has side length 2.0 cm. What is its volume with proper sig figs?
Joe: 2.0 * 2.0 * 2.0 = 8.0 cubic cm. Each measurement has 2 sig figs, so the answer has 2 sig figs.
User: What is its surface area?
Joe: Surface area = 6 * 2.0^2 = 6 * 4.0 = 24. The measurement has 2 sig figs, and 6 is exact. So 24 sq cm.

User: A phone plan costs $30 per month for the first 2 GB and $10 per GB after. You use 5 GB. What is the bill?
Joe: First 2 GB: $30. Additional: 5 - 2 = 3 GB at $10 each = $30. Total = 30 + 30 = $60.
User: What is the average cost per GB?
Joe: $60 / 5 GB = $12 per GB.

User: How much does the air in a typical room weigh?
Joe: A 5 m by 5 m by 3 m room has volume 75 cubic meters. Air density is about 1.2 kg per cubic meter. So the air weighs about 90 kg, which is about the weight of an adult human.
User: How much oxygen is in that room?
Joe: Oxygen is about 21% of air by volume. 75 * 0.21 = 15.75 cubic meters of oxygen. At 1.4 kg per cubic meter, that is about 22 kg of oxygen.

User: What is the order of magnitude of a typical wavelength of visible light?
Joe: Visible light has wavelengths from about 400 nm to 700 nm. 400 nm = 4 * 10^-7 m. The order of magnitude is 10^-7 m.
User: How does that compare to a radio wave?
Joe: A typical FM radio wave has a wavelength of about 3 meters, which is 10^0 m. Visible light is 10^7 times smaller than a radio wave.

User: Estimate the number of seconds in 100 years.
Joe: 100 years * 365 days * 24 hours * 3600 seconds = 3,153,600,000 seconds. Including leap years, about 100 * 365.25 * 86400 = 3,155,760,000 seconds.
User: That is about pi * 10^9 seconds, right?
Joe: Yes, pi is about 3.1416, so pi * 10^9 = 3.1416 billion seconds, which is close to the number of seconds in a century.

User: A rectangle has area 96 and length 12. What is its width and perimeter?
Joe: Width = 96 / 12 = 8. Perimeter = 2(12 + 8) = 2 * 20 = 40.
User: If you increase the length by 3 and decrease the width by 2, what is the new area?
Joe: New length = 15, new width = 6. New area = 15 * 6 = 90.

User: Round 1.0499 to one decimal place.
Joe: 1.0499 to one decimal place: look at the hundredths digit, which is 4. Since 4 < 5, round down. The answer is 1.0.
User: What about 1.05?
Joe: 1.05 to one decimal place: the hundredths digit is 5, so round up. The answer is 1.1.

User: How loud is a typical rock concert in decibels?
Joe: A rock concert is about 110 to 120 decibels. Decibels are logarithmic, so 120 dB is 10 times more intense than 110 dB.
User: How long can you be exposed without hearing damage?
Joe: At 110 dB, hearing damage can occur after about 1 minute. At 85 dB, safe exposure is about 8 hours. The rule is that every 3 dB doubles the sound intensity.

User: A boat can travel 30 km downstream in 3 hours and the same distance upstream in 5 hours. What is the speed of the boat in still water?
Joe: Downstream speed = 30/3 = 10 km/h. Upstream speed = 30/5 = 6 km/h. Boat speed = (10 + 6) / 2 = 8 km/h.
User: What is the speed of the current?
Joe: Current = (10 - 6) / 2 = 2 km/h.

User: A news report says a car gets 100 miles per gallon. Is that plausible?
Joe: Yes, several production cars achieve over 100 mpg, including hybrids and electric vehicles with gas generators. The Toyota Prius gets about 55 mpg, but plug-in hybrids can exceed 100 mpg.
User: What about 200 mpg?
Joe: 200 mpg is plausible for a very efficient plug-in hybrid. The Volkswagen XL1 achieved 260 mpg. However, most 200+ mpg claims are based on electric-only driving for part of the trip.

User: A cylinder has radius 2.5 cm and height 6.0 cm. What is its volume with proper sig figs? Use pi = 3.14.
Joe: Volume = pi * r^2 * h = 3.14 * 6.25 * 6.0 = 3.14 * 37.5 = 117.75. The radius has 2 sig figs, height has 2, and pi is exact for this problem. So the answer should have 2 sig figs: 120 cubic cm.
User: What if pi is not exact?
Joe: If we use pi = 3.14 which has 3 sig figs, the limiting factor is still 2 sig figs from the measurements. The answer remains 120 cubic cm.

User: You have $5,000 invested at 4% simple interest. How much interest per year?
Joe: Interest per year = 5,000 * 0.04 = $200.
User: How long until the investment doubles?
Joe: With simple interest, doubling means earning $5,000 in interest. At $200 per year, that takes 5,000 / 200 = 25 years.

User: How far does a person walk in a lifetime?
Joe: The average person walks about 100,000 miles in a lifetime. That is about 4 times around the Earth.
User: How many steps is that?
Joe: With an average stride of 2.5 feet, 100,000 miles * 5,280 feet per mile / 2.5 feet per step = about 211 million steps.

User: How many dollar bills are in circulation in the US?
Joe: There are about 14 billion dollar bills in circulation. The total value of all US currency in circulation is about $2 trillion.
User: How much does all that cash weigh?
Joe: A dollar bill weighs about 1 gram. 14 billion dollar bills weigh 14 billion grams = 14,000 metric tons.

User: A school has 600 students. 55% are girls and 60% of the boys play soccer. How many boys play soccer?
Joe: Boys = 600 * 0.45 = 270. Boys who play soccer = 270 * 0.60 = 162.
User: What percentage of all students are boys who play soccer?
Joe: 162 / 600 = 0.27 = 27%.

User: A rectangle has length 15 and width 8. A square has the same area. What is the side of the square?
Joe: Rectangle area = 15 * 8 = 120. Square side = sqrt(120) = about 10.95.
User: Which has a larger perimeter?
Joe: Rectangle perimeter = 2(15 + 8) = 46. Square perimeter = 4 * 10.95 = 43.8. The rectangle has a larger perimeter.

User: Is it plausible that a person reads 1,000 words per minute?
Joe: The average reading speed is about 200-300 words per minute for comprehension. Speed readers claim 600-700 wpm with good comprehension. 1,000 wpm is at the edge of plausibility for skimming, but not for detailed comprehension.
User: What about 100 words per minute?
Joe: Yes, 100 wpm is below average but plausible. Children and non-native readers often read at 100-150 wpm.

User: A box of chocolates has 12 pieces. You eat 1/4 of them. How many are left?
Joe: You eat 12 * 1/4 = 3 pieces. Left = 12 - 3 = 9 pieces.
User: If you then eat 1/3 of the remaining, how many are left?
Joe: You eat 9 * 1/3 = 3 more pieces. Left = 9 - 3 = 6 pieces.

User: A rectangular field is 120 m by 80 m. What is its area with proper sig figs?
Joe: 120 has 2 or 3 sig figs, 80 has 1 or 2. Assuming both have 2 sig figs, 120 * 80 = 9,600. With 2 sig figs, the answer is 9,600 sq m or 9.6 * 10^3.
User: What about the perimeter?
Joe: Perimeter = 2(120 + 80) = 400 m. The least precise is 80 with uncertainty in the tens place, so the answer is 400 m.

User: How many hours does a person sleep in a lifetime?
Joe: At 8 hours per night, a person sleeps 8 * 365.25 * 80 = about 233,760 hours, or about 9,740 days, or about 26.7 years.
User: What fraction of a lifetime is that?
Joe: 26.7 / 80 = about 1/3 of a lifetime spent sleeping.

User: How many schools are there in the US?
Joe: There are about 130,000 schools in the US. This includes about 100,000 public schools and 30,000 private schools.
User: How many students per school on average?
Joe: With about 56 million students across 130,000 schools, the average is about 430 students per school.

User: What is the order of magnitude of the age of the universe in years?
Joe: The universe is about 13.8 billion years old, which is 1.38 * 10^10 years. The order of magnitude is 10^10.
User: How many orders of magnitude older is the universe than human civilization?
Joe: Human civilization is about 10,000 years old, which is 10^4 years. The universe is 10^6 times older, or 6 orders of magnitude older.

User: A recipe calls for 1.5 cups of sugar for 24 cookies. How much sugar for 60 cookies?
Joe: 60 / 24 = 2.5 times the recipe. Sugar needed = 1.5 * 2.5 = 3.75 cups.
User: If you only have 2 cups of sugar, how many cookies can you make?
Joe: 2 cups / 1.5 cups per batch = 1.333 batches. 1.333 * 24 = 32 cookies.

User: Estimate the number of words in the English language.
Joe: The Oxford English Dictionary has about 170,000 entries. Including technical terms and slang, the English language has about 500,000 to 1 million words. However, most speakers use only about 20,000 to 30,000 words.
User: How many words does Shakespeare use?
Joe: Shakespeare used about 29,000 different words in his works, of which about 1,700 were his own coinages.
